incompressibleFluid — OpenFOAM incompressible tutorial drop-in

Summary

Case/backend runs: 90   Matched the native reference: 8

OutcomeCountMeaning
SOLVER_FAILED42The neofoam solver crashed or errored; the detail + log say why.
FIELDS_DIFFER15Ran to completion but a field differs beyond tolerance — read max rel: ~1e-13 is round-off, ~1e0 is a real defect.
NATIVE_FAILED12The native reference itself did not run — a harness fault, not a solver verdict.
MATCHED8Reproduced the native reference field-for-field within tolerance.
CASE_SETUP_FAILED6casebuild staging aborted before the run — a harness fault.
UNSUPPORTED_CASE6No unique solver token in Allrun to swap — the case can't be run as a drop-in, so it is outside this study's scope.
COMPARE_FAILED1The run finished but the post-run field read/compare failed — a harness fault.
